# Top GitHub Users By Public Contributions in Finland [<img alt="Image of insights" src="https://github.com/gayanvoice/insights/blob/master/graph/373383893/small/week.png" height="24">](https://github.com/gayanvoice/insights/blob/master/readme/373383893/week.md)

The `public contributions` by users in Finland on `2026/10/18 12:24 AM UTC`. This list contains users from `Finland` and cities `Helsinki` `Tampere` `Oulu` `Espoo` `Vantaa`.

There are `138 countries` and `675 cities` can be found [here](https://github.com/xiv3r/top-github-users-ranking).

There are `921 users`  in Finland. You need at least `54 followers` to be on this list.

🏅 Looking for a shareable ranking badge? Visit the [Finland GitHub user ranking page](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users-ranking/blob/main/markdown/finland.md) to view the country rankings and copy the ready-made badge snippet for your GitHub profile or README.
